CROTON R AT NEW CROTON DAM NR CROTON-ON-HUDSON NY, USGS site 01375000 (USGS-01375000), stream in Westchester County, New York, draining 378 square miles. Daily means from 1933-07-29 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
